			                var sf = $('ul.sf-menu').superfish({
			                    //add options here if required
			                });

								if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
								$('a').each(function () {
								   var link = $(this).attr('href');
								   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){

									   }
								   }

		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});

					    <script type="text/javascript">
		        (function($){ //create closure so we can safely use $ as alias for jQuery
		            $(document).ready(function(){
													var pathname = window.location.href;
							if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
							$('a').each(function () {
							   var link = $(this).attr('href');
							   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){
								   if (link.substr(0,1) == "#" ) {
									   $(this).attr('href', pathname + link);
								   }
							   }
							});
						
		            });
		        })(jQuery);
		    </script>
		
		
		
		
		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

<script>
	function verifyLogin(which) {
		with(which) {
			if(username.value == '' || password.value == ''){
				alert("Please enter your username and password to login");
				return false;
			} else {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
</script>

            // Navigation to open on click for the M2 series layout
            var isTouchStartDetected = false;

            (function($){
                window.onorientationchange = function() {
                    var orientation = window.orientation;

                    switch(orientation) {
                        case 0:
                        case 90:
                        case -90: window.location.reload();
                            break;
                    }
                };

                $(document).ready(function(){
                    $('.sf-with-ul').ready(function(){
                        $('.sf-with-ul, .sf-menu').on('mouseenter mouseout', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });

                        
                        $('.sf-with-ul').on('click touchend', function (e) {
                            if(e.type == 'touchend') {
                                isTouchStartDetected = true;
                            }

                            if(e.type == 'click' && isTouchStartDetected) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            //alert(e.type)
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().removeClass('sfHover');
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().children('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            //$(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');

                            if($(this).parent().hasClass('sfHover')) {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'block'});
                                $(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');
                            } else {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            }
                        })
                    });
                });
            })(jQuery);
        </script>

		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

		        (function($){ //create closure so we can safely use $ as alias for jQuery
		            $(document).ready(function(){
													var pathname = window.location.href;
							if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
							$('a').each(function () {
							   var link = $(this).attr('href');
							   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){
								   if (link.substr(0,1) == "#" ) {
									   $(this).attr('href', pathname + link);
								   }
							   }
							});
						
		            });

		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

<script>
	function verifyLogin(which) {
		with(which) {
			if(username.value == '' || password.value == ''){
				alert("Please enter your username and password to login");
				return false;
			} else {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
</script>

            // Navigation to open on click for the M2 series layout
            var isTouchStartDetected = false;

            (function($){
                window.onorientationchange = function() {
                    var orientation = window.orientation;

                    switch(orientation) {
                        case 0:
                        case 90:
                        case -90: window.location.reload();
                            break;
                    }
                };

                $(document).ready(function(){
                    $('.sf-with-ul').ready(function(){
                        $('.sf-with-ul, .sf-menu').on('mouseenter mouseout', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });

                        
                        $('.sf-with-ul').on('click touchend', function (e) {
                            if(e.type == 'touchend') {
                                isTouchStartDetected = true;
                            }

                            if(e.type == 'click' && isTouchStartDetected) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            //alert(e.type)
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().removeClass('sfHover');
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().children('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            //$(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');

                            if($(this).parent().hasClass('sfHover')) {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'block'});
                                $(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');
                            } else {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            }
                        })
                    });
                });
            })(jQuery);
        </script>

		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

					    <script type="text/javascript">
		        (function($){ //create closure so we can safely use $ as alias for jQuery
		            $(document).ready(function(){
													var pathname = window.location.href;
							if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
							$('a').each(function () {
							   var link = $(this).attr('href');
							   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){
								   if (link.substr(0,1) == "#" ) {
									   $(this).attr('href', pathname + link);
								   }
							   }
							});
						
		            });
		        })(jQuery);
		    </script>
		
		
		
		
		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

<script>
	function verifyLogin(which) {
		with(which) {
			if(username.value == '' || password.value == ''){
				alert("Please enter your username and password to login");
				return false;
			} else {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
</script>

            // Navigation to open on click for the M2 series layout
            var isTouchStartDetected = false;

            (function($){
                window.onorientationchange = function() {
                    var orientation = window.orientation;

                    switch(orientation) {
                        case 0:
                        case 90:
                        case -90: window.location.reload();
                            break;
                    }
                };

                $(document).ready(function(){
                    $('.sf-with-ul').ready(function(){
                        $('.sf-with-ul, .sf-menu').on('mouseenter mouseout', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });

                        
                        $('.sf-with-ul').on('click touchend', function (e) {
                            if(e.type == 'touchend') {
                                isTouchStartDetected = true;
                            }

                            if(e.type == 'click' && isTouchStartDetected) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            //alert(e.type)
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().removeClass('sfHover');
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().children('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            //$(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');

                            if($(this).parent().hasClass('sfHover')) {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'block'});
                                $(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');
                            } else {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            }
                        })
                    });
                });
            })(jQuery);
        </script>

					    <script type="text/javascript">
		        (function($){ //create closure so we can safely use $ as alias for jQuery
		            $(document).ready(function(){
													var pathname = window.location.href;
							if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
							$('a').each(function () {
							   var link = $(this).attr('href');
							   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){
								   if (link.substr(0,1) == "#" ) {
									   $(this).attr('href', pathname + link);
								   }
							   }
							});
						
		            });
		        })(jQuery);
		    </script>
		
		
		
		
		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

<script>
	function verifyLogin(which) {
		with(which) {
			if(username.value == '' || password.value == ''){
				alert("Please enter your username and password to login");
				return false;
			} else {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
</script>

            // Navigation to open on click for the M2 series layout
            var isTouchStartDetected = false;

            (function($){
                window.onorientationchange = function() {
                    var orientation = window.orientation;

                    switch(orientation) {
                        case 0:
                        case 90:
                        case -90: window.location.reload();
                            break;
                    }
                };

                $(document).ready(function(){
                    $('.sf-with-ul').ready(function(){
                        $('.sf-with-ul, .sf-menu').on('mouseenter mouseout', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });

                        
                        $('.sf-with-ul').on('click touchend', function (e) {
                            if(e.type == 'touchend') {
                                isTouchStartDetected = true;
                            }

                            if(e.type == 'click' && isTouchStartDetected) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            //alert(e.type)
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().removeClass('sfHover');
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().children('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            //$(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');

                            if($(this).parent().hasClass('sfHover')) {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'block'});
                                $(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');
                            } else {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            }
                        })
                    });
                });
            })(jQuery);
        </script>

	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);

			                // initialise plugin
			                var sf = $('ul.sf-menu').superfish({
			                    //add options here if required
			                });

															var pathname = window.location.href;
								if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
								$('a').each(function () {
								   var link = $(this).attr('href');
								   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){
									   if (link.substr(0,1) == "#" ) {

									   }
								   }
								});

			            });
			        })(jQuery);

		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

							text_id_3.style.display='none';
						}else{
							text_id_3.style.display='block';
						}
						text_id_3.innerHTML=banner_DESC_3[current_banner_3];

                }
                setTimeout("rotate_banner_3()", time_shown_3);
        }
        function goto_banner_3()
        {
			                window.open( banner_URL_3[current_banner_3]);
			        }

    setTimeout("rotate_banner_3()", 10);

        // -->
</SCRIPT>

		        (function($){ //create closure so we can safely use $ as alias for jQuery
		            $(document).ready(function(){
													var pathname = window.location.href;
							if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
							$('a').each(function () {
							   var link = $(this).attr('href');
							   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){
								   if (link.substr(0,1) == "#" ) {
									   $(this).attr('href', pathname + link);
								   }
							   }
							});
						
		            });
		        })(jQuery);
		    </script>
		
		
		
		
		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

<script>
	function verifyLogin(which) {
		with(which) {
			if(username.value == '' || password.value == ''){
				alert("Please enter your username and password to login");
				return false;
			} else {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
</script>

            var isTouchStartDetected = false;

            (function($){
                window.onorientationchange = function() {
                    var orientation = window.orientation;

                    switch(orientation) {
                        case 0:
                        case 90:
                        case -90: window.location.reload();
                            break;
                    }

                $(document).ready(function(){
                    $('.sf-with-ul').ready(function(){
                        $('.sf-with-ul, .sf-menu').on('mouseenter mouseout', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });

                        
                        $('.sf-with-ul').on('click touchend', function (e) {
                            if(e.type == 'touchend') {
                                isTouchStartDetected = true;
                            }

                            if(e.type == 'click' && isTouchStartDetected) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            //alert(e.type)
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().removeClass('sfHover');
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().children('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            //$(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');

                            if($(this).parent().hasClass('sfHover')) {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'block'});
                                $(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');
                            } else {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            }
                        })
                    });
                });
            })(jQuery);
        </script>

					    <script type="text/javascript">
		        (function($){ //create closure so we can safely use $ as alias for jQuery
		            $(document).ready(function(){
													var pathname = window.location.href;
							if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
							$('a').each(function () {
							   var link = $(this).attr('href');
							   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){
								   if (link.substr(0,1) == "#" ) {
									   $(this).attr('href', pathname + link);
								   }
							   }
							});
						
		            });
		        })(jQuery);
		    </script>
		
		
		
		
		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

<script>
	function verifyLogin(which) {
		with(which) {
			if(username.value == '' || password.value == ''){
				alert("Please enter your username and password to login");
				return false;
			} else {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
</script>

            // Navigation to open on click for the M2 series layout
            var isTouchStartDetected = false;

            (function($){
                window.onorientationchange = function() {
                    var orientation = window.orientation;

                    switch(orientation) {
                        case 0:
                        case 90:
                        case -90: window.location.reload();
                            break;
                    }
                };

                $(document).ready(function(){
                    $('.sf-with-ul').ready(function(){
                        $('.sf-with-ul, .sf-menu').on('mouseenter mouseout', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });

                        
                        $('.sf-with-ul').on('click touchend', function (e) {
                            if(e.type == 'touchend') {
                                isTouchStartDetected = true;
                            }

                            if(e.type == 'click' && isTouchStartDetected) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            //alert(e.type)
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().removeClass('sfHover');
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().children('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            //$(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');

                            if($(this).parent().hasClass('sfHover')) {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'block'});
                                $(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');
                            } else {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            }
                        })
                    });
                });
            })(jQuery);
        </script>

		        (function($){ //create closure so we can safely use $ as alias for jQuery
		            $(document).ready(function(){
													var pathname = window.location.href;
							if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
							$('a').each(function () {
							   var link = $(this).attr('href');
							   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){
								   if (link.substr(0,1) == "#" ) {
									   $(this).attr('href', pathname + link);
								   }
							   }
							});
						
		            });
		        })(jQuery);
		    </script>
		
		
		
		
		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

<script>
	function verifyLogin(which) {
		with(which) {
			if(username.value == '' || password.value == ''){
				alert("Please enter your username and password to login");
				return false;
			} else {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
</script>

            // Navigation to open on click for the M2 series layout
            var isTouchStartDetected = false;

            (function($){
                window.onorientationchange = function() {
                    var orientation = window.orientation;

                    switch(orientation) {
                        case 0:
                        case 90:
                        case -90: window.location.reload();
                            break;
                    }
                };

                $(document).ready(function(){
                    $('.sf-with-ul').ready(function(){
                        $('.sf-with-ul, .sf-menu').on('mouseenter mouseout', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });

                        
                        $('.sf-with-ul').on('click touchend', function (e) {
                            if(e.type == 'touchend') {
                                isTouchStartDetected = true;
                            }

                            if(e.type == 'click' && isTouchStartDetected) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            //alert(e.type)
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().removeClass('sfHover');
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().children('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            //$(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');

                            if($(this).parent().hasClass('sfHover')) {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'block'});
                                $(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');
                            } else {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            }
                        })
                    });
                });
            })(jQuery);
        </script>

		        (function($){ //create closure so we can safely use $ as alias for jQuery
		            $(document).ready(function(){
													var pathname = window.location.href;
							if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
							$('a').each(function () {
							   var link = $(this).attr('href');
							   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){
								   if (link.substr(0,1) == "#" ) {
									   $(this).attr('href', pathname + link);
								   }
							   }
							});
						
		            });
		        })(jQuery);
		    </script>
		
		
		
		
		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

<script>
	function verifyLogin(which) {
		with(which) {
			if(username.value == '' || password.value == ''){
				alert("Please enter your username and password to login");
				return false;
			} else {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
</script>

            // Navigation to open on click for the M2 series layout
            var isTouchStartDetected = false;

            (function($){
                window.onorientationchange = function() {
                    var orientation = window.orientation;

                    switch(orientation) {
                        case 0:
                        case 90:
                        case -90: window.location.reload();
                            break;
                    }
                };

                $(document).ready(function(){
                    $('.sf-with-ul').ready(function(){
                        $('.sf-with-ul, .sf-menu').on('mouseenter mouseout', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });

                        
                        $('.sf-with-ul').on('click touchend', function (e) {
                            if(e.type == 'touchend') {
                                isTouchStartDetected = true;
                            }

                            if(e.type == 'click' && isTouchStartDetected) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            //alert(e.type)
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().removeClass('sfHover');
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().children('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            //$(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');

                            if($(this).parent().hasClass('sfHover')) {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'block'});
                                $(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');
                            } else {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            }
                        })
                    });
                });
            })(jQuery);
        </script>

					    <script type="text/javascript">
		        (function($){ //create closure so we can safely use $ as alias for jQuery
		            $(document).ready(function(){
													var pathname = window.location.href;
							if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
							$('a').each(function () {
							   var link = $(this).attr('href');
							   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){
								   if (link.substr(0,1) == "#" ) {
									   $(this).attr('href', pathname + link);
								   }
							   }
							});
						
		            });
		        })(jQuery);
		    </script>
		
		
		
		
		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

<script>
	function verifyLogin(which) {
		with(which) {
			if(username.value == '' || password.value == ''){
				alert("Please enter your username and password to login");
				return false;
			} else {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
</script>

            // Navigation to open on click for the M2 series layout
            var isTouchStartDetected = false;

            (function($){
                window.onorientationchange = function() {
                    var orientation = window.orientation;

                    switch(orientation) {
                        case 0:
                        case 90:
                        case -90: window.location.reload();
                            break;
                    }
                };

                $(document).ready(function(){
                    $('.sf-with-ul').ready(function(){
                        $('.sf-with-ul, .sf-menu').on('mouseenter mouseout', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });

                        
                        $('.sf-with-ul').on('click touchend', function (e) {
                            if(e.type == 'touchend') {
                                isTouchStartDetected = true;
                            }

                            if(e.type == 'click' && isTouchStartDetected) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            //alert(e.type)
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().removeClass('sfHover');
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().children('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            //$(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');

                            if($(this).parent().hasClass('sfHover')) {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'block'});
                                $(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');
                            } else {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            }
                        })
                    });
                });
            })(jQuery);
        </script>

					    <script type="text/javascript">
		        (function($){ //create closure so we can safely use $ as alias for jQuery
		            $(document).ready(function(){
													var pathname = window.location.href;
							if(pathname.search(/#/)!=-1) pathname=pathname.slice(0,pathname.search(/#/));
							$('a').each(function () {
							   var link = $(this).attr('href');
							   if(typeof(link)!='undefined'){
								   if (link.substr(0,1) == "#" ) {
									   $(this).attr('href', pathname + link);
								   }
							   }
							});
						
		            });
		        })(jQuery);
		    </script>
		
		
		
		
		<script type ="text/javascript">
	var mobile_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var tablet_hamburger_enabled = true;
	var main_navbar_selector = '#main-navbar .sf-menu.sf-js-enabled';
	var window_width = jQuery(window).width(), window_height = jQuery(window).height();

	function hamburgerServiceTrigger() {
	    var size = window.getComputedStyle(document.body,':after').content.replace(/"|'/g, '');

	    if (size == 'mobile' && !mobile_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else if (size == 'tablet' && !tablet_hamburger_enabled) {
	        jQuery(main_navbar_selector).addClass('show');
	    } else {
	    	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).removeClass('show');
	    }
	}

	jQuery(window).on('resize', function(){
		if(jQuery(window).width() != window_width || jQuery(window).height() != window_height){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}
	});

	jQuery(main_navbar_selector).ready(function(){
		setTimeout(function(){
			hamburgerServiceTrigger();
		}, 500);	
	});
</script>

<script>
	function verifyLogin(which) {
		with(which) {
			if(username.value == '' || password.value == ''){
				alert("Please enter your username and password to login");
				return false;
			} else {
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
</script>

            <script>
            // Navigation to open on click for the M2 series layout
            var isTouchStartDetected = false;

            (function($){
                window.onorientationchange = function() {
                    var orientation = window.orientation;

                    switch(orientation) {
                        case 0:
                        case 90:
                        case -90: window.location.reload();
                            break;
                    }
                };

                $(document).ready(function(){
                    $('.sf-with-ul').ready(function(){
                        $('.sf-with-ul, .sf-menu').on('mouseenter mouseout', function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); });

                        
                        $('.sf-with-ul').on('click touchend', function (e) {
                            if(e.type == 'touchend') {
                                isTouchStartDetected = true;
                            }

                            if(e.type == 'click' && isTouchStartDetected) {
                                return false;
                            }

                            //alert(e.type)
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().removeClass('sfHover');
                            //$(this).parent().siblings().children('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            //$(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');

                            if($(this).parent().hasClass('sfHover')) {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'block'});
                                $(this).parent().toggleClass('sfHover');
                            } else {
                                $(this).siblings('.sf-menu').css({display: 'none'});
                            }
                        })
                    });
                });
            })(jQuery);
        </script>
